    10	## About Laravel
    11	
    12	Laravel is a web application framework with expressive, elegant syntax. We believe development must be an enjoyable and creative experience to be truly fulfilling. Laravel takes the pain out of development by easing common tasks used in many web projects, such as:
    13	
    14	- [Simple, fast routing engine](https://laravel.com/docs/routing).
    15	- [Powerful dependency injection container](https://laravel.com/docs/container).
    16	- Multiple back-ends for [session](https://laravel.com/docs/session) and [cache](https://laravel.com/docs/cache) storage.
    17	- Expressive, intuitive [database ORM](https://laravel.com/docs/eloquent).
    18	- Database agnostic [schema migrations](https://laravel.com/docs/migrations).
    19	- [Robust background job processing](https://laravel.com/docs/queues).
    20	- [Real-time event broadcasting](https://laravel.com/docs/broadcasting).
    21	
    22	Laravel is accessible, powerful, and provides tools required for large, robust applications.
    23	
    24	## Learning Laravel
    25	
    26	Laravel has the most extensive and thorough [documentation](https://laravel.com/docs) and video tutorial library of all modern web application frameworks, making it a breeze to get started with the framework. You can also check out [Laravel Learn](https://laravel.com/learn), where you will be guided through building a modern Laravel application.
    27	
    28	If you don't feel like reading, [Laracasts](https://laracasts.com) can help. Laracasts contains thousands of video tutorials on a range of topics including Laravel, modern PHP, unit testing, and JavaScript. Boost your skills by digging into our comprehensive video library.
    29

    10	    case Ordered = 'ordered';
    11	    case Printing = 'printing';
    12	    case Shipped = 'shipped';
    13	    case Cancelled = 'cancelled';
    14	    case Completed = 'completed';
    15	
    16	    public static function values(): array {
    17	        return array_map(fn(self $c) => $c->value, self::cases());
    18	    }
    19	}

============================================================
END FILE: app/Enums/PrintJobStatus.php

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Http\Requests\StoreMaterialRequest;
     7	use App\Http\Requests\UpdateMaterialRequest;
     8	use App\Models\Material;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    10	
    11	class MaterialController extends Controller
    12	{
    13	    //CRUD
    14	    public function index(){
    15	        $materials = Material::query()
    16	            ->latest()
    17	            ->paginate(15);
    18	        return view('admin.materials.index', compact('materials'));
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    public function create(){
    22	        return view('admin.materials.create');
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    public function store(StoreMaterialRequest $request){
    26	        $validated = $request->validated();
    27	
    28	        $validated['slug'] = $this->generateUniqueSlug($validated['name']);
    29	        $validated['is_active'] = (bool) ($validated['is_active'] ?? false);
    30	
    31	        Material::create($validated);
    32	
    33	        return redirect()
    34	            ->route('admin.materials.index')
    35	            ->with('status', 'Material creado correctamente');
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    public function edit(Material $material){
    39	        return view('admin.materials.edit', compact('material'));
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    public function update(UpdateMaterialRequest $request, Material $material){
    43	        $validated = $request->validated();
    44	        $validated['slug'] = $this->generateUniqueSlug($validated['name'], $material->id);
    45	        $validated['is_active'] = (bool) ($validated['is_active'] ?? false);
    46	
    47	        $material->update($validated);
    48	
    49	        return redirect()
    50	            ->route('admin.materials.index')
    51	            ->with('status', 'Material actualizado correctamente');
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    //Función para comprobar si ya existe el slug de un material y le añade un sufijo si es necesario
    55	    private function generateUniqueSlug(string $name, ?int $ignoreId = null): string
    56	    {
    57	        $baseSlug = Str::slug($name);
    58	        $slug = $baseSlug;
    59	        $counter = 2;
    60	
    61	        while (
    62	        Material::where('slug', $slug)
    63	            ->when($ignoreId, fn ($q) => $q->where('id', '!=', $ignoreId))
    64	            ->exists()
    65	        ) {
    66	            $slug = $baseSlug . '-' . $counter;
    67	            $counter++;
    68	        }
    69	
    70	        return $slug;
    71	    }
    72	}
    73

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PrintFileController.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\PrintFileStatus;
     6	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     7	use App\Http\Requests\StorePrintFileRequest;
     8	use App\Http\Requests\UpdatePrintFileRequest;
     9	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
    10	use App\Models\PrintFile;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
    12	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
    13	
    14	class PrintFileController extends Controller
    15	{
    16	    public function index(Request $request){
    17	        $query = PrintFile::query()
    18	            ->with('user:id,name,last_name,email')
    19	            ->orderBy('id');
    20	
    21	        if ($request->filled('status')){
    22	            $query->where('status', $request->string('status')->toString());
    23	        }
    24	        if ($request->filled('user_id')){
    25	            $query->where('user_id', $request->integer('user_id'));
    26	        }
    27	        $printFiles = $query->paginate(20)->withQueryString();
    28	        return view('admin.print-files.index', compact('printFiles'));
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    public function create()
    32	    {
    33	        return view('admin.print-files.create');
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    public function show(PrintFile $printFile)
    37	    {
    38	        // Admin: acceso total. Si más adelante hay user-panel, aquí se comprobará ownership.
    39	        return view('admin.print-files.show', compact('printFile'));
    40	    }
    41	
    42	
    43	    public function store(StorePrintFileRequest $request)
    44	    {
    45	        $file = $request->file('file');
    46	        $userId = auth()->id();
    47	
    48	        DB::beginTransaction();
    49	
    50	        try {
    51	            //Creo el registro base con el storage_path aún NULL
    52	            $printFile = PrintFile::create([
    53	                'user_id'       => $userId,
    54	                'original_name' => $file->getClientOriginalName(),
    55	                'status'        => PrintFileStatus::Uploaded,
    56	                'notes'         => $request->input('notes'),
    57	            ]);
    58	
    59	            //Construyo la ruta
    60	            $directory = "print-files/{$userId}/{$printFile->id}";
    61	            $filename  = $file->getClientOriginalName();
    62	            $path      = "{$directory}/{$filename}";
    63	
    64	            //Guardar el archivo en storage privado
    65	            Storage::disk('local')->putFileAs(
    66	                $directory,
    67	                $file,
    68	                $filename
    69	            );
    70	
    71	            //Actualizo los metadatos
    72	            $printFile->update([
    73	                'storage_path'   => $path,
    74	                'mime_type'      => $file->getClientMimeType(),
    75	                'file_extension' => $file->getClientOriginalExtension(),
    76	                'file_size'      => $file->getSize(),
    77	            ]);
    78	
    79	            DB::commit();
    80	
    81	            return redirect()
    82	                ->route('admin.print-files.index')
    83	                ->with('success', 'Archivo subido correctamente.');
    84	
    85	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    86	
    87	            DB::rollBack();
    88	
    89	            //Si el archivo no se escribe lo borro todo para que no queden líneas parciales en la BD
    90	            if (isset($path) && Storage::disk('local')->exists($path)) {
    91	                Storage::disk('local')->deleteDirectory($directory);
    92	            }
    93	
    94	            throw $e;
    95	        }
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    public function update(UpdatePrintFileRequest $request, PrintFile $printFile)
    99	    {
   100	        $data = $request->validated();
   101	
   102	        $metadata = null;
   103	        if (!empty($data['metadata'])) {
   104	            $metadata = json_decode($data['metadata'], true);
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        $printFile->update([
   108	            'status' => $data['status'],
   109	            'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
   110	            'metadata' => $metadata,
   111	        ]);
   112	
   113	        return redirect()
   114	            ->route('admin.print-files.show', $printFile)
   115	            ->with('success', 'Archivo actualizado correctamente.');
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    public function edit(PrintFile $printFile)
   119	    {
   120	        $statuses = PrintFileStatus::cases();
   121	
   122	        return view('admin.print-files.edit', compact('printFile', 'statuses'));
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    public function destroy(PrintFile $printFile)
   126	    {
   127	        // Directorio donde debería estar el archivo (seguro incluso si storage_path es null)
   128	        $directory = $printFile->storage_path
   129	            ? dirname($printFile->storage_path)
   130	            : "print-files/{$printFile->user_id}/{$printFile->id}";
   131	
   132	        // Borrado físico (si no existe, no pasa nada)
   133	        Storage::disk('local')->deleteDirectory($directory);
   134	
   135	        // Borrado lógico (SoftDelete)
   136	        $printFile->delete();
   137	
   138	        return redirect()
   139	            ->route('admin.print-files.index')
   140	            ->with('success', 'Archivo eliminado correctamente.');
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    //Descarga del archivo
   144	    public function download(PrintFile $printFile)
   145	    {
   146	        $relativePath = $printFile->storage_path;
   147	
   148	        abort_unless(Storage::disk('local')->exists($relativePath), 404);
   149	
   150	        return Storage::disk('local')->download(
   151	            $relativePath,
   152	            $printFile->original_name
   153	        );
   154	    }
   155	
   156	
   157	}

END FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PrintFileController.php

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\PrintJobStatus;
     6	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     7	use App\Http\Requests\StorePrintJobRequest;
     8	use App\Http\Requests\UpdatePrintJobRequest;
     9	use App\Models\Material;
    10	use App\Models\PrintFile;
    11	use App\Models\PrintJob;
    12	
    13	class PrintJobController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    // GET /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs
    16	    public function index(PrintFile $printFile)
    17	    {
    18	        $printJobs = $printFile->printJobs()
    19	            ->with(['material:id,name'])
    20	            ->latest()
    21	            ->paginate(20);
    22	
    23	        return view('admin.print-files.jobs.index', compact('printFile', 'printJobs'));
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    // GET /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/create
    27	    public function create(PrintFile $printFile)
    28	    {
    29	        $materials = Material::query()
    30	            ->select('id', 'name')
    31	            ->where('is_active', true)
    32	            ->orderBy('name')
    33	            ->get();
    34	
    35	        return view('admin.print-files.jobs.create', compact('printFile', 'materials'));
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    // POST /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs
    39	    public function store(StorePrintJobRequest $request, PrintFile $printFile)
    40	    {
    41	        $data = $request->validated();
    42	
    43	        // Fuente de verdad: ruta anidada
    44	        $data['print_file_id'] = $printFile->id;
    45	
    46	        // El job pertenece al propietario del archivo (no editable desde el formulario)
    47	        $data['user_id'] = $printFile->user_id;
    48	
    49	        $data['status'] = PrintJobStatus::Draft;
    50	
    51	        $printJob = PrintJob::create($data);
    52	
    53	        return redirect()
    54	            ->route('admin.print-files.jobs.show', [$printFile, $printJob])
    55	            ->with('success', 'PrintJob creado correctamente.');
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    // GET /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}
    59	    public function show(PrintFile $printFile, PrintJob $printJob)
    60	    {
    61	        abort_unless((int) $printJob->print_file_id === (int) $printFile->id, 404);
    62	
    63	        $printJob->load([
    64	            'material:id,name',
    65	            'printFile:id,original_name,user_id',
    66	        ]);
    67	
    68	        return view('admin.print-files.jobs.show', compact('printFile', 'printJob'));
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    // GET /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}/edit
    72	    public function edit(PrintFile $printFile, PrintJob $printJob)
    73	    {
    74	        abort_unless((int) $printJob->print_file_id === (int) $printFile->id, 404);
    75	
    76	        $status = $printJob->status?->value ?? (string) $printJob->status;
    77	
    78	        if ($status !== 'draft') {
    79	            return redirect()
    80	                ->route('admin.print-files.jobs.show', [$printFile, $printJob])
    81	                ->with('error', 'Este PrintJob no se puede editar en su estado actual.');
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        $materials = Material::query()
    85	            ->select('id', 'name')
    86	            ->where('is_active', true)
    87	            ->orderBy('name')
    88	            ->get();
    89	
    90	        return view('admin.print-files.jobs.edit', compact('printFile', 'printJob', 'materials'));
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    // PUT /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}
    94	    public function update(UpdatePrintJobRequest $request, PrintFile $printFile, PrintJob $printJob)
    95	    {
    96	        abort_unless((int) $printJob->print_file_id === (int) $printFile->id, 404);
    97	
    98	        $status = $printJob->status?->value ?? (string) $printJob->status;
    99	
   100	        if ($status !== 'draft') {
   101	            return redirect()
   102	                ->route('admin.print-files.jobs.show', [$printFile, $printJob])
   103	                ->with('error', 'Este PrintJob no se puede actualizar en su estado actual (ya está en el carrito).');
   104	        }
   105	
   106	
   107	        $data = $request->validated();
   108	
   109	        //Blindaje: estos campos no se reasignan desde update
   110	        unset($data['print_file_id'], $data['user_id']);
   111	
   112	        $printJob->update($data);
   113	
   114	        return redirect()
   115	            ->route('admin.print-files.jobs.show', [$printFile, $printJob])
   116	            ->with('success', 'PrintJob actualizado correctamente.');
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    // DELETE /admin/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}
   120	    public function destroy(PrintFile $printFile, PrintJob $printJob)
   121	    {
   122	        abort_unless((int) $printJob->print_file_id === (int) $printFile->id, 404);
   123	
   124	        $printJob->delete();
   125	
   126	        return redirect()
   127	            ->route('admin.print-files.jobs.index', $printFile)
   128	            ->with('success', 'PrintJob eliminado correctamente.');
   129	    }
   130	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4

     7

    13	class ProductController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    //vista de productos
    16	    public function index(){
    17	        $products = Product::query()
    18	            ->with(['mainImage']) //Carga de antemano la relación de la imagen principal (así solo hace dos consultas)
    19	            ->withCount('variants') //Atributo virtual, me permite hacer $product->variant_count en la vista
    20	            ->latest()
    21	            ->get();
    22	
    23	        return view('admin.products.index', compact("products"));
    24	    }

    26	    public function create(){
    27	        return view('admin.products.create');
    28	    }

    30	    public function store(Request $request){
    31	        $validated = $request->validate([
    32	            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
    33	            'description' => ['required', 'string'],
    34	            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
    35	        ]);
    36	
    37	        //para la checkbox
    38	        $validated['is_active'] = (bool) ($validated['is_active'] ?? false);
    39	        $validated['slug'] = str::slug($validated['name']);
    40	        $validated['type'] = 'footwear';
    41	
    42	        Product::create($validated);
    43	
    44	        return redirect()->route('admin.products.index')->with('status', 'Producto creado correctamente');
    45	    }

    50	    }

    54	            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
    55	            'description' => ['required', 'string'],
    56	            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
    57	        ]);
    58	        $validated['is_active'] = (bool) ($validated['is_active'] ?? false);
    59	        $validated['slug'] = str::slug($validated['name']);
    60	
    61	        $product->update($validated);
    62	
    63	        return redirect()->route('admin.products.index')->with('status', 'Producto actualizado correctamente');
    64	    }
    65	    public function destroy(Product $product)
    66	    {
    67	        $product->delete(); // soft delete
    68	
    69	        return redirect()
    70	            ->route('admin.products.index')
    71	            ->with('status', 'Producto eliminado correctamente');
    72	    }
    73	
    74	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ProductImageController.php

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\Product;
     7	use App\Models\ProductImage;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     9	use App\Http\Requests\StoreProductImageRequest;
    10	use App\Http\Requests\UpdateProductImageRequest;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
    12	
    13	class ProductImageController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    public function index(Product $product){
    16	        $images = $product->images()
    17	            ->orderBy('sort_order')
    18	            ->get();
    19	        return view('admin.products.images.index', compact('product', 'images'));
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    public function create(Product $product){
    23	        return view('admin.products.images.create', compact('product'));
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public function store(StoreProductImageRequest $request, Product $product)
    27	    {
    28	        $validated = $request->validated();
    29	
    30	        $isMain = (bool) ($validated['is_main'] ?? false);
    31	
    32	        if ($isMain) {
    33	            $product->images()->update(['is_main' => false]);
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        $nextOrder = ($product->images()->max('sort_order') ?? 0) + 1;
    37	
    38	        $path = $request->file('image')->store('products/' . $product->id, 'public');
    39	
    40	        $product->images()->create([
    41	            'path' => $path,
    42	            'alt_text' => $validated['alt_text'] ?? null,
    43	            'is_main' => $isMain,
    44	            'sort_order' => $nextOrder,
    45	        ]);
    46	
    47	        return redirect()
    48	            ->route('admin.products.images.index', $product)
    49	            ->with('status', 'Imagen subida correctamente');
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    public function edit(Product $product, ProductImage $image){
    53	        abort_unless($image->product_id === $product->id, 404);
    54	        return view('admin.products.images.edit', compact('product', 'image'));
    55	
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    public function update(UpdateProductImageRequest $request, Product $product, ProductImage $image)
    59	    {
    60	        abort_unless($image->product_id === $product->id, 404);
    61	
    62	        $validated = $request->validated();
    63	
    64	        //Reemplazo de archivo
    65	        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
    66	            if ($image->path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($image->path)) {
    67	                Storage::disk('public')->delete($image->path);
    68	            }
    69	
    70	            $image->path = $request->file('image')
    71	                ->store('products/' . $product->id, 'public');
    72	        }
    73	
    74	        //Texto alternativo
    75	        $image->alt_text = $validated['alt_text'] ?? null;
    76	
    77	        // 3) Imagen principal (solo si se marca)
    78	        $isMain = (bool) ($validated['is_main'] ?? false);
    79	        if ($isMain) {
    80	            $product->images()->whereKeyNot($image->id)->update(['is_main' => false]);
    81	            $image->is_main = true;
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        $image->save();
    85	
    86	        return redirect()
    87	            ->route('admin.products.images.index', $product)
    88	            ->with('status', 'Imagen actualizada correctamente');
    89	    }
    90	
    91	
    92	    public function destroy(Product $product, ProductImage $image)
    93	    {
    94	        abort_unless($image->product_id === $product->id, 404);
    95	
    96	        // Borrado físico del archivo
    97	        if ($image->path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($image->path)) {
    98	            Storage::disk('public')->delete($image->path);
    99	        }
   100	
   101	        $image->delete();
   102	
   103	        return redirect()
   104	            ->route('admin.products.images.index', $product)
   105	            ->with('status', 'Imagen eliminada correctamente');
   106	    }
   107	
   108	
   109	}

    52	    {

    54	         * Validación eliminada al crear las request
    55	         *
    56	        $validated = $request->validate([
    57	            'sku' => ['required', 'string', 'max:64', 'unique:product_variants,sku'],
    58	            'size_eu' => ['required', 'numeric'],
    59	            'color_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
    60	            'material_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:materials,id'],
    61	            'price' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
    62	            'stock' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:0'],
    63	            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
    64	        ]);

    66	        $validated = $request->validated();

    68

    70	        return redirect()

    93	            'material_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:materials,id'],
    94	            'sku' => ['required', 'string', 'max:64', 'unique:product_variants,sku,' . $variant->id],
    95	            'size_eu' => ['required', 'numeric'],
    96	            'color_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
    97	            'price' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
    98	            'stock' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:0'],
    99	            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
   100	        ]);
   101	        */
   102	        $validated = $request->validated();

   104

    49

    51	            $variant->images()->update(['is_main' => false]);
    52	        }
    53	
    54	        $nextOrder = ($variant->images()->max('sort_order') ?? 0) + 1;
    55

    57	            'products/' . $product->id . '/variants/' . $variant->id,

    66	        ]);

    79	
    80	        // Reemplazo de archivo (opcional)
    81	        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
    82	            if ($image->path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($image->path)) {
    83	                Storage::disk('public')->delete($image->path);
    84	            }
    85	
    86	            $image->path = $request->file('image')
    87	                ->store('products/' . $product->id . '/variants/' . $variant->id, 'public');
    88	        }
    89	
    90	        // Alt text
    91	        $image->alt_text = $validated['alt_text'] ?? null;
    92	
    93	        // Principal
    94	        $isMain = (bool) ($validated['is_main'] ?? false);
    95	        if ($isMain) {
    96	            $variant->images()->whereKeyNot($image->id)->update(['is_main' => false]);
    97	            $image->is_main = true;
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        $image->save();
   101	
   102	        return redirect()
   103	            ->route('admin.products.variants.images.index', [$product, $variant])
   104	            ->with('status', 'Imagen de variante actualizada correctamente');
   105	    }

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Admin/UserCartController.php

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Http\Requests\Carts\AddPrintJobToCartRequest;
     7	use App\Http\Requests\Carts\AddProductVariantToCartRequest;
     8	use App\Http\Requests\Carts\CheckoutCartRequest;
     9	use App\Models\Address;
    10	use App\Models\Cart;
    11	use App\Models\CartItem;
    12	use App\Models\Order;
    13	use App\Models\OrderItem;
    14	use App\Models\PrintJob;
    15	use App\Models\ProductVariant;
    16	use App\Models\User;
    17	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
    18	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    19	use App\Services\PrintJobPricingService;
    20	
    21	class UserCartController extends Controller
    22	{
    23	    public function show(User $user)
    24	    {
    25	        // Busco el carrito activo del usuario (y precargo relaciones para renderizar la vista).
    26	        // Uso latest por si más adelante permito histórico de carritos.
    27	        $cart = Cart::query()
    28	            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
    29	            ->where('status', 'active')
    30	            ->with(['items.productVariant', 'items.printJob'])
    31	            ->latest('id')
    32	            ->first();
    33	
    34	        return view('admin.users.cart.show', compact('user', 'cart'));
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    // Para añadir un ProductVariant al carrito
    38	    public function addProductVariant(AddProductVariantToCartRequest $request, User $user, ProductVariant $variant)
    39	    {
    40	        $quantityToAdd = (int) $request->validated()['quantity'];
    41	
    42	        // Garantizo que existe un carrito activo para este usuario.
    43	        $cart = Cart::query()->firstOrCreate(
    44	            [
    45	                'user_id' => $user->id,
    46	                'status'  => 'active',
    47	            ],
    48	            []
    49	        );
    50	
    51	        // Precio unitario del variant (si no existe aún, lo dejo en 0 por seguridad).
    52	        $unitPrice = (string) data_get($variant, 'price', '0.00');
    53	
    54	        DB::transaction(function () use ($cart, $variant, $quantityToAdd, $unitPrice) {
    55	            // Bloqueo la posible línea existente para evitar carreras si se envían dos requests a la vez.
    56	            $item = CartItem::query()
    57	                ->where('cart_id', $cart->id)
    58	                ->where('product_variant_id', $variant->id)
    59	                ->whereNull('print_job_id')
    60	                ->lockForUpdate()
    61	                ->first();
    62	
    63	            if ($item) {
    64	                $newQty = $item->quantity + $quantityToAdd;
    65	
    66	                $item->update([
    67	                    'quantity'   => $newQty,
    68	                    'unit_price' => $unitPrice,
    69	                    'subtotal'   => $this->moneyMul($unitPrice, $newQty),
    70	                ]);
    71	
    72	                return;
    73	            }
    74	
    75	            CartItem::query()->create([
    76	                'cart_id'            => $cart->id,
    77	                'product_variant_id' => $variant->id,
    78	                'print_job_id'       => null,
    79	                'quantity'           => $quantityToAdd,
    80	                'unit_price'         => $unitPrice,
    81	                'subtotal'           => $this->moneyMul($unitPrice, $quantityToAdd),
    82	                'metadata'           => [
    83	                    'source' => 'product_variant',
    84	                ],
    85	            ]);
    86	        });
    87	
    88	        return redirect()
    89	            ->route('admin.users.cart.show', $user)
    90	            ->with('success', 'Producto añadido al carrito.');
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    // Para añadir un PrintJob al carrito
    94	    public function addPrintJob(AddPrintJobToCartRequest $request, User $user, PrintJob $printJob)
    95	    {
    96	        //El PrintJob debe pertenecer a ese usuario.
    97	        abort_unless($printJob->user_id === $user->id, 404);
    98	
    99	        //El estado actual (enum o string)
   100	        $status = $printJob->status?->value ?? (string) $printJob->status;
   101	
   102	        //Solo se permiten estos estados para esta operación así lo protejo mejor
   103	        abort_unless(in_array($status, ['draft', 'priced', 'in_cart'], true), 422);
   104	
   105	        //Me aseguro y garantizo que existe un carrito activo.
   106	        $cart = Cart::query()->firstOrCreate(
   107	            [
   108	                'user_id' => $user->id,
   109	                'status'  => 'active',
   110	            ],
   111	            []
   112	        );
   113	
   114	        $quantityToAdd = $request->validatedQuantity();
   115	
   116	        DB::transaction(function () use ($cart, $printJob, $quantityToAdd, &$status) {
   117	
   118	            //Bloqueo el PrintJob
   119	            $printJob->lockForUpdate();
   120	
   121	            /*
   122	             * Si está en draft, aquí calculo el precio,
   123	             * se persiste el snapshot y se pasa a priced.
   124	             */
   125	            if ($status === 'draft') {
   126	                $pricingService = app(PrintJobPricingService::class);
   127	
   128	                $quote = $pricingService->quote($printJob);
   129	
   130	                $printJob->update([
   131	                    'estimated_material_g' => $quote['estimated_material_g'],
   132	                    'estimated_time_min'   => $quote['estimated_time_min'],
   133	                    'unit_price'           => $quote['unit_price'],
   134	                    'pricing_breakdown'    => $quote['pricing_breakdown'],
   135	                    'status'               => 'priced',
   136	                ]);
   137	
   138	                $status = 'priced';
   139	            }
   140	
   141	            //priced/in_cart siempre debe tener precio
   142	            abort_unless(!is_null($printJob->unit_price), 422, 'El PrintJob no tiene unit_price.');
   143	
   144	            //Si entra al carrito por primera vez, reflejo el estado
   145	            if ($status === 'priced') {
   146	                $printJob->status = 'in_cart';
   147	                $printJob->save();
   148	            }
   149	
   150	            $unitPrice = (string) $printJob->unit_price;
   151	
   152	            //Busco si ya existe línea para este PrintJob
   153	            $existing = CartItem::query()
   154	                ->where('cart_id', $cart->id)
   155	                ->where('print_job_id', $printJob->id)
   156	                ->whereNull('product_variant_id')
   157	                ->lockForUpdate()
   158	                ->first();
   159	
   160	            if ($existing) {
   161	                $newQty = $existing->quantity + $quantityToAdd;
   162	
   163	                $existing->update([
   164	                    'quantity'   => $newQty,
   165	                    'unit_price' => $unitPrice,
   166	                    'subtotal'   => $this->moneyMul($unitPrice, $newQty),
   167	                ]);
   168	
   169	                return;
   170	            }
   171	
   172	            CartItem::query()->create([
   173	                'cart_id'            => $cart->id,
   174	                'print_job_id'       => $printJob->id,
   175	                'product_variant_id' => null,
   176	                'quantity'           => $quantityToAdd,
   177	                'unit_price'         => $unitPrice,
   178	                'subtotal'           => $this->moneyMul($unitPrice, $quantityToAdd),
   179	                'metadata'           => [
   180	                    'source' => 'print_job',
   181	                ],
   182	            ]);
   183	        });
   184	
   185	        return redirect()
   186	            ->route('admin.users.cart.show', $user)
   187	            ->with('success', 'PrintJob añadido al carrito.');
   188	    }
   189	
   190	
   191	    // Método para convertir un Cart en un Order
   192	    public function checkout(CheckoutCartRequest $request, User $user)
   193	    {
   194	        // Valido direcciones primero (si vienen en request).
   195	        $shippingAddressId = $request->shippingAddressId();
   196	        $billingAddressId  = $request->billingAddressId();
   197	
   198	        if ($shippingAddressId !== null) {
   199	            $shipping = Address::query()->findOrFail($shippingAddressId);
   200	            abort_unless($shipping->user_id === $user->id, 404);
   201	        }
   202	
   203	        if ($billingAddressId !== null) {
   204	            $billing = Address::query()->findOrFail($billingAddressId);
   205	            abort_unless($billing->user_id === $user->id, 404);
   206	        }
   207	
   208	        $paymentMethod = $request->paymentMethod();
   209	        $notes = $request->notes();
   210	
   211	        return DB::transaction(function () use (
   212	            $user,
   213	            $shippingAddressId,
   214	            $billingAddressId,
   215	            $paymentMethod,
   216	            $notes
   217	        ) {
   218	            // En checkout bloqueo el carrito activo para evitar que cambie mientras genero el pedido.
   219	            $cart = Cart::query()
   220	                ->where('user_id', $user->id)
   221	                ->where('status', 'active')
   222	                ->lockForUpdate()
   223	                ->first();
   224	
   225	            abort_if(!$cart, 404, 'El usuario no tiene carrito activo.');
   226	
   227	            // Cargo los items ya dentro de la transacción y con lock.
   228	            $items = CartItem::query()
   229	                ->where('cart_id', $cart->id)
   230	                ->lockForUpdate()
   231	                ->get();
   232	
   233	            abort_if($items->isEmpty(), 422, 'El carrito está vacío.');
   234	
   235	            // Integridad previa: validar líneas del carrito antes de crear el pedido.
   236	            foreach ($items as $item) {
   237	                $isProduct = !is_null($item->product_variant_id);
   238	                $isPrint   = !is_null($item->print_job_id);
   239	
   240	                // XOR obligatorio
   241	                abort_unless($isProduct xor $isPrint, 500);
   242	
   243	                // Snapshot mínimo del carrito (BD lo fuerza, pero validamos igual)
   244	                abort_unless(!is_null($item->unit_price), 422, 'Hay una línea sin precio unitario.');
   245	                abort_unless(!is_null($item->subtotal), 422, 'Hay una línea sin subtotal.');
   246	
   247	                if ($isPrint) {
   248	                    $printJob = PrintJob::query()
   249	                        ->whereKey($item->print_job_id)
   250	                        ->lockForUpdate()
   251	                        ->first();
   252	
   253	                    abort_unless($printJob, 422, 'El PrintJob del carrito ya no existe.');
   254	                    abort_unless((int) $printJob->user_id === (int) $user->id, 404);
   255	
   256	                    $pjStatus = $printJob->status?->value ?? (string) $printJob->status;
   257	                    abort_unless(in_array($pjStatus, ['in_cart'], true), 422, 'El PrintJob no está listo para checkout.');
   258	
   259	                    // Coherencia adicional (recomendable)
   260	                    abort_unless(!is_null($printJob->unit_price), 422, 'El PrintJob no tiene unit_price calculado.');
   261	                }
   262	            }
   263	
   264	
   265	            // Cálculos básicos (de momento sin IVA ni envío).
   266	            $subtotal = (float) $items->sum(fn (CartItem $i) => (float) $i->subtotal);
   267	            $tax = 0.00;
   268	            $shippingCost = 0.00;
   269	            $total = $subtotal + $tax + $shippingCost;
   270	
   271	            // Si no se especifican direcciones, tiro de una “por defecto”.
   272	            $shippingId = $shippingAddressId ?? $this->defaultUserAddressId($user, 'shipping');
   273	            $billingId  = $billingAddressId ?? $this->defaultUserAddressId($user, 'billing');
   274	
   275	            $order = Order::query()->create([
   276	                'user_id'             => $user->id,
   277	                'shipping_address_id' => $shippingId,
   278	                'billing_address_id'  => $billingId,
   279	                'order_number'        => $this->generateOrderNumber(),
   280	                'status'              => 'pending',
   281	                'payment_status'      => 'pending',
   282	                'payment_method'      => $paymentMethod,
   283	                'subtotal'            => $this->money($subtotal),
   284	                'tax'                 => $this->money($tax),
   285	                'shipping_cost'       => $this->money($shippingCost),
   286	                'total'               => $this->money($total),
   287	                'placed_at'           => now(),
   288	                'notes'               => $notes,
   289	            ]);
   290	
   291	            // Convierto cada CartItem en OrderItem.
   292	            foreach ($items as $item) {
   293	                $isProduct = !is_null($item->product_variant_id);
   294	                $isPrint   = !is_null($item->print_job_id);
   295	
   296	                // Integridad: una línea es o producto o printjob, pero no ambos.
   297	                abort_unless($isProduct xor $isPrint, 500);
   298	
   299	                $itemName = $isProduct
   300	                    ? ('Variant #' . $item->product_variant_id)
   301	                    : ('PrintJob #' . $item->print_job_id);
   302	
   303	                OrderItem::query()->create([
   304	                    'order_id'           => $order->id,
   305	                    'product_variant_id' => $item->product_variant_id,
   306	                    'print_job_id'       => $item->print_job_id,
   307	                    'item_name'          => $itemName,
   308	                    'unit_price'         => $item->unit_price,
   309	                    'quantity'           => $item->quantity,
   310	                    'subtotal'           => $item->subtotal,
   311	                    'metadata'           => $item->metadata,
   312	                ]);
   313	
   314	                // Si es PrintJob, lo marco como ordered para mantener coherencia con el ciclo.
   315	                if ($isPrint) {
   316	                    $printJob = PrintJob::query()->whereKey($item->print_job_id)->lockForUpdate()->first();
   317	                    if ($printJob) {
   318	                        $status = $printJob->status?->value ?? (string) $printJob->status;
   319	                        if (in_array($status, ['draft', 'priced', 'in_cart'], true)) {
   320	                            $printJob->status = 'ordered';
   321	                            $printJob->save();
   322	                        }
   323	                    }
   324	                }
   325	            }
   326	
   327	            // Cierro el carrito.
   328	            $cart->status = 'ordered';
   329	            $cart->save();
   330	
   331	            return redirect()
   332	                ->route('admin.orders.show', $order)
   333	                ->with('success', 'Carrito convertido en pedido.');
   334	        });
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    private function moneyMul(string|float $unitPrice, int $quantity): string
   338	    {
   339	        $value = (float) $unitPrice * $quantity;
   340	        return number_format(round($value, 2), 2, '.', '');
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    private function generateOrderNumber(): string
   344	    {
   345	        return 'ORD-' . Str::upper(Str::random(10));
   346	    }
   347	
   348	    private function money(float $value): string
   349	    {
   350	        return number_format(round($value, 2), 2, '.', '');
   351	    }
   352	
   353	    private function defaultUserAddressId(User $user, string $type): int
   354	    {
   355	        // Si tienes address_type en la tabla, lo uso para coger una por defecto coherente.
   356	        // Si no existiera (o aún no lo estás usando), puedes simplificar a ->first().
   357	        $address = $user->addresses()
   358	            ->where('address_type', $type)
   359	            ->first();
   360	
   361	        if (!$address) {
   362	            $address = $user->addresses()->first();
   363	        }
   364	
   365	        abort_unless($address, 422, 'El usuario no tiene direcciones registradas.');
   366	        return (int) $address->id;
   367	    }
   368	}

============================================================
END FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Admin/UserCartController.php

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Api/AuthController.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     9	use Illuminate\Http\Response;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
    12	
    13	class AuthController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    public function login(LoginRequest $request): Response
    16	    {
    17	        $request->authenticate();
    18	        $request->session()->regenerate();
    19	
    20	        return response()
    21	            ->noContent()
    22	            ->header('X-Lexxis-Auth', 'api-login');
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    public function logout(Request $request): Response
    26	    {
    27	        Auth::guard('web')->logout();
    28	
    29	        $request->session()->invalidate();
    30	        $request->session()->regenerateToken();
    31	
    32	        return response()->noContent();
    33	    }
    34	
    35	
    36	    public function tokenLogin(Request $request)
    37	    {
    38	        $data = $request->validate([
    39	            'email'    => ['required', 'email'],
    40	            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
    41	        ]);
    42	
    43	        $user = User::where('email', $data['email'])->first();
    44	
    45	        if (!$user || !Hash::check($data['password'], $user->password)) {
    46	            return response()->json([
    47	                'message' => 'Credenciales incorrectas',
    48	            ], 401);
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        if (!$user->is_active) {
    52	            return response()->json([
    53	                'message' => 'Usuario inactivo',
    54	            ], 403);
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        //Limpieza de tokens anteriores
    58	        $user->tokens()->delete();
    59	
    60	        $token = $user->createToken('postman-token')->plainTextToken;
    61	
    62	        return response()->json([
    63	            'token' => $token,
    64	            'token_type' => 'Bearer',
    65	            'user' => [
    66	                'id' => $user->id,
    67	                'name' => $user->name,
    68	                'email' => $user->email,
    69	                'role' => $user->role ?? null,
    70	            ],
    71	        ]);
    72	    }
    73	
    74	}
    75

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api\Catalog;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\Product;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	
     9	class ProductController extends Controller
    10	{
    11	    public function index(Request $request)
    12	    {
    13	        $query = Product::query()
    14	            ->where('is_active', true)
    15	            ->with(['mainImage'])
    16	            ->latest();
    17	
    18	        if ($request->filled('q')) {
    19	            $q = trim((string) $request->query('q'));
    20	            $query->where(function ($sub) use ($q) {
    21	                $sub->where('name', 'like', "%{$q}%")
    22	                    ->orWhere('slug', 'like', "%{$q}%")
    23	                    ->orWhere('type', 'like', "%{$q}%");
    24	            });
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        $products = $query->paginate(15);
    28	
    29	        return response()->json([
    30	            'data' => $products->items(),
    31	            'meta' => [
    32	                'current_page' => $products->currentPage(),
    33	                'per_page' => $products->perPage(),
    34	                'total' => $products->total(),
    35	                'last_page' => $products->lastPage(),
    36	            ],
    37	        ]);
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function show(Product $product)
    41	    {
    42	        abort_unless($product->is_active, 404);
    43	
    44	        $product->load([
    45	            'mainImage',
    46	            'variants' => function ($q) {
    47	                $q->where('is_active', true)
    48	                    ->with(['material', 'mainImage'])
    49	                    ->latest();
    50	            },
    51	        ]);
    52	
    53	        return response()->json([
    54	            'data' => $product,
    55	        ]);
    56	    }
    57	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\PrintFileStatus;
     6	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     7	use App\Http\Requests\StorePrintFileRequest;
     8	use App\Models\PrintFile;
     9	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
    12	
    13	class PrintFileController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    public function index(Request $request)
    16	    {
    17	        $userId = $request->user()->id;
    18	
    19	        $query = PrintFile::query()
    20	            ->where('user_id', $userId)
    21	            ->orderByDesc('id');
    22	
    23	        if ($request->filled('status')) {
    24	            $query->where('status', (string) $request->query('status'));
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        $printFiles = $query->paginate(20);
    28	
    29	        return response()->json([
    30	            'data' => $printFiles->items(),
    31	            'meta' => [
    32	                'current_page' => $printFiles->currentPage(),
    33	                'per_page' => $printFiles->perPage(),
    34	                'total' => $printFiles->total(),
    35	                'last_page' => $printFiles->lastPage(),
    36	            ],
    37	        ]);
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function show(Request $request, PrintFile $printFile)
    41	    {
    42	        abort_unless($printFile->user_id === $request->user()->id, 403);
    43	
    44	        return response()->json([
    45	            'data' => $printFile,
    46	        ]);
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    public function store(StorePrintFileRequest $request)
    50	    {
    51	        $file = $request->file('file');
    52	        $userId = $request->user()->id;
    53	
    54	        DB::beginTransaction();
    55	
    56	        try {
    57	            $printFile = PrintFile::create([
    58	                'user_id'       => $userId,
    59	                'original_name' => $file->getClientOriginalName(),
    60	                'status'        => PrintFileStatus::Uploaded,
    61	                'notes'         => $request->input('notes'),
    62	                'storage_path'  => '', // se actualiza después
    63	            ]);
    64	
    65	            $directory = "print-files/{$userId}/{$printFile->id}";
    66	            $filename  = $file->getClientOriginalName();
    67	            $path      = "{$directory}/{$filename}";
    68	
    69	            Storage::disk('local')->putFileAs($directory, $file, $filename);
    70	
    71	            $printFile->update([
    72	                'storage_path'   => $path,
    73	                'mime_type'      => $file->getClientMimeType(),
    74	                'file_extension' => $file->getClientOriginalExtension(),
    75	                'file_size'      => $file->getSize(),
    76	            ]);
    77	
    78	            DB::commit();
    79	
    80	            return response()->json([
    81	                'message' => 'Archivo subido correctamente',
    82	                'data' => $printFile,
    83	            ], 201);
    84	
    85	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    86	            DB::rollBack();
    87	
    88	            if (isset($path) && Storage::disk('local')->exists($path)) {
    89	                Storage::disk('local')->deleteDirectory($directory);
    90	            }
    91	
    92	            throw $e;
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    public function download(Request $request, PrintFile $printFile)
    97	    {
    98	        abort_unless($printFile->user_id === $request->user()->id, 403);
    99	
   100	        $relativePath = $printFile->storage_path;
   101	        abort_unless($relativePath && Storage::disk('local')->exists($relativePath), 404);
   102	
   103	        return Storage::disk('local')->download($relativePath, $printFile->original_name);
   104	    }
   105	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
    10	use Illuminate\View\View;
    11	use Illuminate\Http\Response;
    12	
    13	class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
    14	{
    15	    /**
    16	     * Display the login view.
    17	     */
    18	    public function create(): View
    19	    {
    20	        return view('auth.login');
    21	    }
    22	
    23	    /**
    24	     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
    25	     */
    26	    public function store(LoginRequest $request): Response|RedirectResponse
    27	    {
    28	        $request->authenticate();
    29	
    30	        $request->session()->regenerate();
    31	
    32	        if ($request->wantsJson() || $request->expectsJson()) {
    33	            return response()->noContent();
    34	        }
    35	
    36	
    37	        // Breeze/Blade -> flujo habitual
    38	        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    /**
    42	     * Destroy an authenticated session.
    43	     */
    44	    public function destroy(Request $request): Response|RedirectResponse
    45	    {
    46	        Auth::guard('web')->logout();
    47	
    48	        $request->session()->invalidate();
    49	        $request->session()->regenerateToken();
    50	
    51	        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
    52	            return response()->noContent();
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        return redirect('/');
    56	    }
    57	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
     9	use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
    10	use Illuminate\View\View;
    11	
    12	class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * Show the confirm password view.
    16	     */
    17	    public function show(): View
    18	    {
    19	        return view('auth.confirm-password');
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    /**
    23	     * Confirm the user's password.
    24	     */
    25	    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    26	    {
    27	        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
    28	            'email' => $request->user()->email,
    29	            'password' => $request->password,
    30	        ])) {
    31	            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
    32	                'password' => __('auth.password'),
    33	            ]);
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());
    37	
    38	        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    39	    }
    40	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     9	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
    12	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    13	use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
    14	use Illuminate\View\View;
    15	
    16	class NewPasswordController extends Controller
    17	{
    18	    /**
    19	     * Display the password reset view.
    20	     */
    21	    public function create(Request $request): View
    22	    {
    23	        return view('auth.reset-password', ['request' => $request]);
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    /**
    27	     * Handle an incoming new password request.
    28	     *
    29	     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
    30	     */
    31	    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    32	    {
    33	        $request->validate([
    34	            'token' => ['required'],
    35	            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
    36	            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
    37	        ]);
    38	
    39	        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
    40	        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
    41	        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
    42	        $status = Password::reset(
    43	            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
    44	            function (User $user) use ($request) {
    45	                $user->forceFill([
    46	                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
    47	                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
    48	                ])->save();
    49	
    50	                event(new PasswordReset($user));
    51	            }
    52	        );
    53	
    54	        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
    55	        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
    56	        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
    57	        return $status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET
    58	                    ? redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status))
    59	                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
    60	                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    61	    }
    62	}

============================================================
END FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php
============================================================

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordController.php

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
     9	use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;
    10	
    11	class PasswordController extends Controller
    12	{
    13	    /**
    14	     * Update the user's password.
    15	     */
    16	    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    17	    {
    18	        $validated = $request->validateWithBag('updatePassword', [
    19	            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
    20	            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
    21	        ]);
    22	
    23	        $request->user()->update([
    24	            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
    25	        ]);
    26	
    27	        return back()->with('status', 'password-updated');
    28	    }
    29	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
     9	use Illuminate\View\View;
    10	
    11	class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
    12	{
    13	    /**
    14	     * Display the password reset link request view.
    15	     */
    16	    public function create(): View
    17	    {
    18	        return view('auth.forgot-password');
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    /**
    22	     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
    23	     *
    24	     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
    25	     */
    26	    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    27	    {
    28	        $request->validate([
    29	            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
    30	        ]);
    31	
    32	        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
    33	        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
    34	        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
    35	        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
    36	            $request->only('email')
    37	        );
    38	
    39	        return $status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT
    40	                    ? back()->with('status', __($status))
    41	                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
    42	                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    43	    }
    44	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     9	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
    11	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
    12	use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
    13	use Illuminate\View\View;
    14	
    15	class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
    16	{
    17	    /**
    18	     * Display the registration view.
    19	     */
    20	    public function create(): View
    21	    {
    22	        return view('auth.register');
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    /**
    26	     * Handle an incoming registration request.
    27	     *
    28	     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
    29	     */
    30	    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    31	    {
    32	        $request->validate([
    33	            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
    34	            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'lowercase', 'email', 'max:255', 'unique:'.User::class],
    35	            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
    36	        ]);
    37	
    38	        $user = User::create([
    39	            'name' => $request->name,
    40	            'email' => $request->email,
    41	            'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
    42	        ]);
    43	
    44	        event(new Registered($user));
    45	
    46	        Auth::login($user);
    47	
    48	        return redirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    49	    }
    50	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     9	
    10	class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
    11	{
    12	    /**
    13	     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
    14	     */
    15	    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    16	    {
    17	        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
    18	            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    19	        }
    20	
    21	        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
    22	            event(new Verified($request->user()));
    23	        }
    24	
    25	        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    26	    }
    27	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Controllers/ProfileController.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Requests\ProfileUpdateRequest;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Redirect;
    10	use Illuminate\View\View;
    11	
    12	class ProfileController extends Controller
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * Display the user's profile form.
    16	     */
    17	    public function edit(Request $request): View
    18	    {
    19	        return view('profile.edit', [
    20	            'user' => $request->user(),
    21	        ]);
    22	    }
    23	
    24	    /**
    25	     * Update the user's profile information.
    26	     */
    27	    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    28	    {
    29	        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());
    30	
    31	        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
    32	            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        $request->user()->save();
    36	
    37	        return Redirect::route('profile.edit')->with('status', 'profile-updated');
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    /**
    41	     * Delete the user's account.
    42	     */
    43	    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    44	    {
    45	        $request->validateWithBag('userDeletion', [
    46	            'password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
    47	        ]);
    48	
    49	        $user = $request->user();
    50	
    51	        Auth::logout();
    52	
    53	        $user->delete();
    54	
    55	        $request->session()->invalidate();
    56	        $request->session()->regenerateToken();
    57	
    58	        return Redirect::to('/');
    59	    }
    60	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Middleware/EnsureUserIsAdmin.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Middleware;
     4	
     5	use Closure;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     7	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
     8	
     9	class EnsureUserIsAdmin
    10	{
    11	    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    12	    {
    13	        $user = $request->user();
    14	
    15	        if (!$user || !$user->is_active || !$user->isAdmin()) {
    16	            abort(403);
    17	        }
    18	
    19	        return $next($request);
    20	    }
    21	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Requests/Auth/LoginRequest.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Requests\Auth;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Lockout;
     6	use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
     8	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    10	use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
    11	
    12	class LoginRequest extends FormRequest
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    16	     */
    17	    public function authorize(): bool
    18	    {
    19	        return true;
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    /**
    23	     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
    24	     *
    25	     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
    26	     */
    27	    public function rules(): array
    28	    {
    29	        return [
    30	            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email'],
    31	            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
    32	        ];
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    /**
    36	     * Attempt to authenticate the request's credentials.
    37	     *
    38	     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
    39	     */
    40	    public function authenticate(): void
    41	    {
    42	        $this->ensureIsNotRateLimited();
    43	
    44	        if (! Auth::attempt($this->only('email', 'password'), $this->boolean('remember'))) {
    45	            RateLimiter::hit($this->throttleKey());
    46	
    47	            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
    48	                'email' => trans('auth.failed'),
    49	            ]);
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        RateLimiter::clear($this->throttleKey());
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    /**
    56	     * Ensure the login request is not rate limited.
    57	     *
    58	     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
    59	     */
    60	    public function ensureIsNotRateLimited(): void
    61	    {
    62	        if (! RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($this->throttleKey(), 5)) {
    63	            return;
    64	        }
    65	
    66	        event(new Lockout($this));
    67	
    68	        $seconds = RateLimiter::availableIn($this->throttleKey());
    69	
    70	        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
    71	            'email' => trans('auth.throttle', [
    72	                'seconds' => $seconds,
    73	                'minutes' => ceil($seconds / 60),
    74	            ]),
    75	        ]);
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    /**
    79	     * Get the rate limiting throttle key for the request.
    80	     */
    81	    public function throttleKey(): string
    82	    {
    83	        return Str::transliterate(Str::lower($this->string('email')).'|'.$this->ip());
    84	    }
    85	}

============================================================
END FILE: app/Http/Requests/Auth/LoginRequest.php
============================================================

     7	class CheckoutCartRequest extends FormRequest
     8	{
     9	    /**
    10	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    11	     */
    12	    public function authorize(): bool
    13	    {
    14	        return true;
    15	    }
    16	
    17	    /**

    23	    {

    41	    }
    42	
    43	    public function paymentMethod(): ?string
    44	    {
    45	        return $this->validated('payment_method');
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    public function notes(): ?string
    49	    {

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Requests;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
     6	use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
     7	
     8	class UpdateAddressRequest extends FormRequest
     9	{
    10	    /**
    11	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    12	     */
    13	    public function authorize(): bool
    14	    {
    15	        return auth()->check() && auth()->user()->isAdmin();
    16	    }
    17	
    18	    /**
    19	     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
    20	     *
    21	     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
    22	     */
    23	    public function rules(): array
    24	    {
    25	        return [
    26	            'alias' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
    27	
    28	            'street_id' => ['required', 'integer', Rule::exists('streets', 'id')],
    29	
    30	            'street_number' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
    31	            'floor' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
    32	            'door' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
    33	
    34	            'address_type' => ['required', Rule::in(['shipping', 'billing'])],
    35	
    36	            'contact_phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
    37	        ];
    38	    }
    39	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Requests/UpdateOrderRequest.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Requests;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\OrderStatus;
     6	use App\Enums\PaymentStatus;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
     8	use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
     9	
    10	class UpdateOrderRequest extends FormRequest
    11	{
    12	    /**
    13	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    14	     */
    15	    public function authorize(): bool
    16	    {
    17	        return auth()->check() && auth()->user()->isAdmin();
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    /**
    21	     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
    22	     *
    23	     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
    24	     */
    25	    public function rules(): array
    26	    {
    27	        return [
    28	            'status' => ['required', Rule::in(array_column(OrderStatus::cases(), 'value'))],
    29	            'payment_status' => ['required', Rule::in(array_column(PaymentStatus::cases(), 'value'))],
    30	            'payment_method' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
    31	            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
    32	        ];
    33	    }
    34	    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    35	    {
    36	        if ($this->has('payment_method')) {
    37	            $value = trim((string) $this->payment_method);
    38	
    39	            $this->merge([
    40	                'payment_method' => $value === '' ? null : $value,
    41	            ]);
    42	        }
    43	
    44	        if ($this->has('notes')) {
    45	            $value = trim((string) $this->notes);
    46	
    47	            $this->merge([
    48	                'notes' => $value === '' ? null : $value,
    49	            ]);
    50	        }
    51	    }
    52	
    53	}

============================================================
END FILE: app/Http/Requests/UpdateOrderRequest.php
============================================================

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Requests/UpdateProductVariantRequest.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Requests;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
     6	
     7	class UpdateProductVariantRequest extends FormRequest
     8	{
     9	    /**
    10	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    11	     */
    12	    public function authorize(): bool
    13	    {
    14	
    15	        return auth()->check();
    16	    }
    17	
    18	    /**
    19	     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
    20	     *
    21	     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
    22	     */
    23	    public function rules(): array
    24	    {
    25	        $variantId = $this->route('variant')?->id ?? $this->route('variant');
    26	        return [
    27	            'material_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:materials,id' ],
    28	            'sku' => ['required', 'string', 'max:64', 'unique:product_variants,sku,' .$variantId],
    29	            'size_eu' => ['required', 'numeric'],
    30	            'color_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
    31	            'price' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
    32	            'stock' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:0'],
    33	            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
    34	        ];
    35	    }
    36	
    37	}

BEGIN FILE: app/Http/Requests/UpdateUserRequest.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Requests;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\UserRole;
     6	use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
     7	use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
     8	
     9	class UpdateUserRequest extends FormRequest
    10	{
    11	    /**
    12	     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
    13	     */
    14	    public function authorize(): bool
    15	    {
    16	        return auth()->check() && auth()->user()->isAdmin();
    17	    }
    18	
    19	    /**
    20	     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
    21	     *
    22	     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
    23	     */
    24	    public function rules(): array
    25	    {
    26	        /** @var \App\Models\User $user */
    27	        $user = $this->route('user');
    28	
    29	        return [
    30	            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
    31	
    32	            'last_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
    33	
    34	            'email' => [
    35	                'required',
    36	                'email',
    37	                'max:255',
    38	                Rule::unique('users', 'email')->ignore($user->id),
    39	            ],
    40	
    41	            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
    42	
    43	            'role' => [
    44	                'required',
    45	                Rule::in(array_column(UserRole::cases(), 'value')),
    46	            ],
    47	
    48	            'is_active' => ['required', 'boolean'],
    49	        ];
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    53	    {
    54	        if ($this->has('email')) {
    55	            $this->merge([
    56	                'email' => strtolower(trim($this->email)),
    57	            ]);
    58	        }
    59	    }
    60	}

    31	        'subtotal'       => 'decimal:2',

    37	        'payment_status' => PaymentStatus::class,
    38	    ];

BEGIN FILE: app/Models/User.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Models;
     4	
     5	// use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
     6	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
     8	use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;
     9	
    10	use Laravel\Sanctum\HasApiTokens;
    11	
    12	class User extends Authenticatable
    13	{
    14	
    15	    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\UserFactory> */
    16	    use HasFactory, Notifiable, HasApiTokens;
    17	
    18	    /**
    19	     * The attributes that are mass assignable.
    20	     *
    21	     * @var list<string>
    22	     */
    23	    protected $fillable = [
    24	        'name',
    25	        'last_name',
    26	        'email',
    27	        'phone',
    28	        'role',
    29	        'is_active',
    30	        'password',
    31	    ];
    32	
    33	    /**
    34	     * The attributes that should be hidden for serialization.
    35	     *
    36	     * @var list<string>
    37	     */
    38	    protected $hidden = [
    39	        'password',
    40	        'remember_token',
    41	    ];
    42	
    43	    /**
    44	     * Get the attributes that should be cast.
    45	     *
    46	     * @return array<string, string>
    47	     */
    48	    protected function casts(): array
    49	    {
    50	        return [
    51	            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
    52	            'password' => 'hashed',
    53	            'is_active' => 'boolean',
    54	            'role' => \App\Enums\UserRole::class,
    55	        ];
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    /**
    59	     * Aunque el valor por defecto en la migración ya es customer, sobreescribo el método
    60	     * para reforzar la instrucción
    61	     */
    62	    protected static function boot()
    63	    {
    64	        parent::boot();
    65	
    66	        static::creating(function ($user) {
    67	            if (!$user->role){
    68	                $user->role = \App\Enums\UserRole::CUSTOMER;
    69	            }
    70	        });
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    //Para poder ver la lista de Addresses de un usuario usando $user->addresses
    74	    public function addresses(){
    75	        return $this->hasMany(Address::class);
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    //para comprobar el rol del usuario
    79	    public function isAdmin(): bool {
    80	        return $this->role === \App\Enums\UserRole::ADMIN;
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    public function isCustomer(): bool {
    84	        return $this->role === \App\Enums\UserRole::CUSTOMER;
    85	    }
    86	    public function cart()
    87	    {
    88	        return $this->hasOne(\App\Models\Cart::class);
    89	    }
    90	
    91	    public function carts()
    92	    {
    93	        return $this->hasMany(\App\Models\Cart::class);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	
    97	}
