BEGIN FILE: app/Services/PrintJobPricingService.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Services;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\PricingSetting;
     6	use App\Models\PrintJob;
     7	
     8	class PrintJobPricingService
     9	{
    10	    public function quote(PrintJob $printJob, int $infill = 15): array
    11	    {
    12	        $pricing = PricingSetting::active();
    13	        abort_unless($pricing, 500, 'No hay PricingSetting activa.');
    14	
    15	        $infillMultipliers = (array) ($pricing->infill_multipliers ?? []);
    16	        $infillMultiplier = (float) ($infillMultipliers[(string) $infill] ?? 1.0);
    17	
    18	        //MVP: si no hay estimaciones todavía, uso placeholders seguros
    19	        //En el siguiente paso conecto esto con métricas reales (STL/OBJ)
    20	        $materialG = (float) ($printJob->estimated_material_g ?? 0);
    21	        $timeMin   = (int) ($printJob->estimated_time_min ?? 0);
    22	
    23	        $materialCostPerG = ((float) $pricing->material_cost_per_kg) / 1000.0;
    24	        $materialCost = $materialG * $materialCostPerG * (float) $pricing->material_multiplier * $infillMultiplier;
    25	
    26	        $machineCost = $timeMin * (float) $pricing->machine_cost_per_min * (float) $pricing->time_multiplier * $infillMultiplier;
    27	
    28	        $setupFee = (float) $pricing->setup_fee_per_job;
    29	
    30	        $base = $materialCost + $machineCost + $setupFee;
    31	        $unitPrice = $base * (float) $pricing->margin_multiplier;
    32	
    33	        $unitPrice = round($unitPrice, 2);
    34	
    35	        return [
    36	            'estimated_material_g' => round($materialG, 2),
    37	            'estimated_time_min' => $timeMin,
    38	            'unit_price' => number_format($unitPrice, 2, '.', ''),
    39	            'pricing_breakdown' => [
    40	                'version' => (string) $pricing->version,
    41	                'inputs' => [
    42	                    'material_id' => (int) $printJob->material_id,
    43	                    'technology' => (string) $printJob->technology,
    44	                    'infill' => $infill,
    45	                    'quantity' => (int) $printJob->quantity,
    46	                ],
    47	                'estimation' => [
    48	                    'material_g' => round($materialG, 2),
    49	                    'time_min' => $timeMin,
    50	                ],
    51	                'costs' => [
    52	                    'material' => round($materialCost, 2),
    53	                    'machine_time' => round($machineCost, 2),
    54	                    'setup_fee' => round($setupFee, 2),
    55	                ],
    56	                'multipliers' => [
    57	                    'material' => (float) $pricing->material_multiplier,
    58	                    'time' => (float) $pricing->time_multiplier,
    59	                    'margin' => (float) $pricing->margin_multiplier,
    60	                    'infill' => $infillMultiplier,
    61	                ],
    62	                'unit_price' => number_format($unitPrice, 2, '.', ''),
    63	            ],
    64	        ];
    65	    }
    66	}
    67	

============================================================
END FILE: app/Services/PrintJobPricingService.php
============================================================

BEGIN FILE: bootstrap/app.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
     4	use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Exceptions;
     5	use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Middleware;
     6	use Laravel\Sanctum\Http\Middleware\EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful;
     7	
     8	return Application::configure(basePath: dirname(__DIR__))
     9	    ->withRouting(
    10	        web: __DIR__.'/../routes/web.php',
    11	        api: __DIR__.'/../routes/api.php',
    12	        commands: __DIR__.'/../routes/console.php',
    13	        health: '/up',
    14	    )
    15	    ->withMiddleware(function (Middleware $middleware): void {
    16	        $middleware->append(\App\Http\Middleware\TrustProxies::class);
    17	
    18	        $middleware->alias([
    19	            'admin' => \App\Http\Middleware\EnsureUserIsAdmin::class,
    20	        ]);
    21	        $middleware->group('api', [
    22	            EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful::class,
    23	            \Illuminate\Routing\Middleware\SubstituteBindings::class,
    24	        ]);
    25	    })
    26	
    27	    ->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $exceptions): void {
    28	    })->create();

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	return [
     4	
     5	    /*
     6	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	    | Authentication Defaults
     8	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9	    |
    10	    | This option defines the default authentication "guard" and password
    11	    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    12	    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    13	    |
    14	    */
    15	
    16	    'defaults' => [
    17	        'guard' => env('AUTH_GUARD', 'web'),
    18	        'passwords' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_BROKER', 'users'),
    19	    ],
    20	
    21	    /*
    22	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23	    | Authentication Guards
    24	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	    |
    26	    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    27	    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    28	    | which utilizes session storage plus the Eloquent user provider.
    29	    |
    30	    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    31	    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    32	    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    33	    |
    34	    | Supported: "session"
    35	    |
    36	    */
    37	
    38	    'guards' => [
    39	        'web' => [
    40	            'driver' => 'session',
    41	            'provider' => 'users',
    42	        ],
    43	    ],
    44	
    45	    /*
    46	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47	    | User Providers
    48	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	    |
    50	    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    51	    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    52	    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    53	    |
    54	    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    55	    | providers to represent the model / table. These providers may then
    56	    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    57	    |
    58	    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    59	    |
    60	    */
    61	
    62	    'providers' => [
    63	        'users' => [
    64	            'driver' => 'eloquent',
    65	            'model' => env('AUTH_MODEL', App\Models\User::class),
    66	        ],
    67	
    68	        // 'users' => [
    69	        //     'driver' => 'database',
    70	        //     'table' => 'users',
    71	        // ],
    72	    ],
    73	
    74	    /*
    75	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76	    | Resetting Passwords
    77	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78	    |
    79	    | These configuration options specify the behavior of Laravel's password
    80	    | reset functionality, including the table utilized for token storage
    81	    | and the user provider that is invoked to actually retrieve users.
    82	    |
    83	    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    84	    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    85	    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    86	    |
    87	    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    88	    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    89	    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    90	    |
    91	    */
    92	
    93	    'passwords' => [
    94	        'users' => [
    95	            'provider' => 'users',
    96	            'table' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TABLE', 'password_reset_tokens'),
    97	            'expire' => 60,
    98	            'throttle' => 60,
    99	        ],
   100	    ],
   101	
   102	    /*
   103	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104	    | Password Confirmation Timeout
   105	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106	    |
   107	    | Here you may define the number of seconds before a password confirmation
   108	    | window expires and users are asked to re-enter their password via the
   109	    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
   110	    |
   111	    */
   112	
   113	    'password_timeout' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_TIMEOUT', 10800),
   114	
   115	];

     3	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
     4	
     5	return [
     6	
     7	    /*
     8	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9	    | Default Session Driver
    10	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11	    |
    12	    | This option determines the default session driver that is utilized for
    13	    | incoming requests. Laravel supports a variety of storage options to
    14	    | persist session data. Database storage is a great default choice.
    15	    |
    16	    | Supported: "file", "cookie", "database", "memcached",
    17	    |            "redis", "dynamodb", "array"
    18	    |
    19	    */
    20	
    21	    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'database'),
    22	
    23	    /*
    24	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	    | Session Lifetime
    26	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27	    |
    28	    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session
    29	    | to be allowed to remain idle before it expires. If you want them
    30	    | to expire immediately when the browser is closed then you may
    31	    | indicate that via the expire_on_close configuration option.
    32	    |
    33	    */
    34	
    35	    'lifetime' => (int) env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),
    36	
    37	    'expire_on_close' => env('SESSION_EXPIRE_ON_CLOSE', false),
    38	
    39	    /*
    40	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41	    | Session Encryption
    42	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43	    |
    44	    | This option allows you to easily specify that all of your session data
    45	    | should be encrypted before it's stored. All encryption is performed
    46	    | automatically by Laravel and you may use the session like normal.
    47	    |
    48	    */
    49	
    50	    'encrypt' => env('SESSION_ENCRYPT', false),
    51

    59	    | are free to provide another location where they should be stored.
    60	    |
    61	    */
    62	
    63	    'files' => storage_path('framework/sessions'),
    64	
    65	    /*
    66	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    73	    |
    74	    */
    75

    85	    | for you; however, you're welcome to change this to another table.
    86	    |

    88

    90

    92	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    96	    | When using one of the framework's cache driven session backends, you may

    98	    | between requests. This must match one of your defined cache stores.
    99	    |
   100	    | Affects: "dynamodb", "memcached", "redis"
   101	    |
   102	    */
   103	
   104	    'store' => env('SESSION_STORE'),
   105	
   106	    /*
   107	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108	    | Session Sweeping Lottery
   109	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

   111	    | Some session drivers must manually sweep their storage location to get

   113	    | happen on a given request. By default, the odds are 2 out of 100.
   114	    |
   115	    */

   118

   158

   195	    |

   197	    |

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Factories;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
     6	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * @extends \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory<\App\Models\User>
    11	 */
    12	class UserFactory extends Factory
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * The current password being used by the factory.
    16	     */
    17	    protected static ?string $password;
    18	
    19	    /**
    20	     * Define the model's default state.
    21	     *
    22	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    23	     */
    24	    public function definition(): array
    25	    {
    26	        return [
    27	            'name' => fake()->name(),
    28	            'last_name' => fake()->lastname(),
    29	            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
    30	            'email_verified_at' => now(),
    31	            'phone' => fake()->phoneNumber(),
    32	            'role' => \App\Enums\UserRole::CUSTOMER,
    33	            'is_active' => true,
    34	            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
    35	            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
    36	        ];
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    /**
    40	     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
    41	     */
    42	    public function unverified(): static
    43	    {
    44	        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
    45	            'email_verified_at' => null,
    46	        ]);
    47	    }
    48	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
     4	use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
     5	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
     6	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
     7	
     8	return new class extends Migration
     9	{
    10	    public function up(): void
    11	    {
    12	        /**
    13	         * Esta migración refuerza la integridad del esquema geográfico (countries, provinces,
    14	         * cities y streets) una vez creado el modelo inicial.
    15	         *
    16	         * El objetivo es evitar duplicados lógicos y permitir la carga segura de catálogos
    17	         * reales mediante seeders (países del mundo, provincias y ciudades de España),
    18	         * sin modificar migraciones ya ejecutadas ni alterar su orden.
    19	         *
    20	         * Se añaden restricciones UNIQUE y se normaliza el uso de claves naturales
    21	         * (por ejemplo, iso_code en countries) para garantizar consistencia a largo plazo.
    22	         */
    23	
    24	        DB::table('countries')
    25	            ->whereNull('iso_code')
    26	            ->update(['iso_code' => 'UNK']);
    27	
    28	        Schema::table('countries', function (Blueprint $table) {
    29	            $table->string('iso_code', 3)->nullable(false)->change();
    30	        });
    31	
    32	        Schema::table('countries', function (Blueprint $table) {

    34	        });
    35

    39

    39	            ['name' => 'Portugal', 'iso_code' => 'PRT'],
    40	            ['name' => 'Spain', 'iso_code' => 'ESP'],
    41	            ['name' => 'Sweden', 'iso_code' => 'SWE'],
    42	            ['name' => 'Switzerland', 'iso_code' => 'CHE'],
    43	            ['name' => 'United Kingdom', 'iso_code' => 'GBR'],
    44	            ['name' => 'United States', 'iso_code' => 'USA'],
    45	        ];
    46	        foreach ($countries as $country) {
    47	            Country::updateOrCreate(
    48	                ['iso_code' => $country['iso_code']],
    49	                ['name' => $country['name']]
    50	            );
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Address;
     6	use App\Models\Street;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     9	
    10	class DemoAddressesSeeder extends Seeder
    11	{
    12	    /**
    13	     * Run the database seeds.
    14	     */
    15	    public function run(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $user = User::where('email', 'demo@lexxis.test')->firstOrFail();
    18	
    19	        // Streets ya existentes (DEV): usamos una de Madrid y otra de Alicante/Alacant si existe
    20	        $streetMadrid = Street::whereHas('city', fn ($q) => $q->where('name', 'Madrid'))->first();
    21	        $streetAlicante = Street::whereHas('city', fn ($q) => $q->where('name', 'Alicante/Alacant'))->first();
    22	
    23	        // Fallback: si no están esas, usamos cualquier street existente para no romper el seeder
    24	        $fallbackStreet = Street::query()->firstOrFail();
    25	
    26	        $streetMadrid = $streetMadrid ?? $fallbackStreet;
    27	        $streetAlicante = $streetAlicante ?? $fallbackStreet;
    28	
    29	        // SHIPPING
    30	        Address::updateOrCreate(
    31	            ['user_id' => $user->id, 'alias' => 'Casa'],
    32	            [
    33	                'street_id' => $streetMadrid->id,
    34	                'street_number' => '10',
    35	                'floor' => '3',
    36	                'door' => 'B',
    37	                'address_type' => 'shipping',
    38	                'contact_phone' => $user->phone,
    39	            ]
    40	        );
    41	
    42	        // BILLING
    43	        Address::updateOrCreate(
    44	            ['user_id' => $user->id, 'alias' => 'Facturación'],
    45	            [
    46	                'street_id' => $streetAlicante->id,
    47	                'street_number' => '5',
    48	                'floor' => null,
    49	                'door' => 'A',
    50	                'address_type' => 'billing',
    51	                'contact_phone' => $user->phone,
    52	            ]
    53	        );
    54	    }
    55	}

BEGIN FILE: database/seeders/DevOrdersSeeder.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Database\Console\Seeds\WithoutModelEvents;
     6	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     7	use App\Models\Address;
     8	use App\Models\Order;
     9	use App\Models\OrderItem;
    10	use App\Models\PrintJob;
    11	use App\Models\ProductVariant;
    12	use App\Models\User;
    13	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    14	
    15	class DevOrdersSeeder extends Seeder
    16	{
    17	    /**
    18	     * Run the database seeds.
    19	     */
    20	    public function run(): void
    21	    {
    22	        $users = User::query()
    23	            ->whereHas('addresses', function ($q) {
    24	                $q->where('address_type', 'shipping');
    25	            })
    26	            ->take(10)
    27	            ->get();
    28	
    29	
    30	        if ($users->isEmpty()) {
    31	            $this->command?->warn('No hay usuarios. DevOrdersSeeder no hizo nada.');
    32	            return;
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        $variants = ProductVariant::query()->pluck('id')->all();
    36	        $jobs     = PrintJob::query()->pluck('id')->all();
    37	
    38	        foreach ($users as $user) {
    39	            //Direcciones del usuario
    40	            $shippingAddresses = Address::query()
    41	                ->where('user_id', $user->id)
    42	                ->where('address_type', 'shipping')
    43	                ->pluck('id')
    44	                ->all();
    45	
    46	            $billingAddresses = Address::query()
    47	                ->where('user_id', $user->id)
    48	                ->where('address_type', 'billing')
    49	                ->pluck('id')
    50	                ->all();
    51	
    52	            if (empty($shippingAddresses)) {
    53	                $this->command?->warn("User {$user->id} sin direcciones shipping. Se omite.");
    54	                continue;
    55	            }
    56	
    57	            $ordersCount = rand(1, 3);
    58	
    59	            for ($i = 0; $i < $ordersCount; $i++) {
    60	                $order = Order::factory()->create([
    61	                    'user_id' => $user->id,
    62	                    'shipping_address_id' => $shippingAddresses[array_rand($shippingAddresses)],
    63	                    'billing_address_id' => !empty($billingAddresses)
    64	                        ? $billingAddresses[array_rand($billingAddresses)]
    65	                        : null,
    66	                    'order_number' => 'ORD-' . strtoupper(Str::random(10)),
    67	                ]);
    68	
    69	                $itemsCount = rand(2, 5);
    70	
    71	                for ($j = 0; $j < $itemsCount; $j++) {
    72	                    $item = OrderItem::factory()->make();
    73	
    74	                    $payload = [
    75	                        'order_id' => $order->id,
    76	                        'item_name' => $item->item_name,
    77	                        'unit_price' => $item->unit_price,
    78	                        'quantity' => $item->quantity,
    79	                        'subtotal' => $item->subtotal,
    80	                        'metadata' => $item->metadata,
    81	                        'product_variant_id' => null,
    82	                        'print_job_id' => null,
    83	                    ];
    84	
    85	                    //Elegimos tipo de item
    86	                    $canUseVariant = !empty($variants);
    87	                    $canUseJob     = !empty($jobs);
    88	
    89	                    if ($canUseVariant && $canUseJob) {
    90	                        // Mezcla: 60% producto / 40% impresión (ajustable)
    91	                        $useVariant = (rand(1, 100) <= 60);
    92	                        if ($useVariant) {
    93	                            $payload['product_variant_id'] = $variants[array_rand($variants)];
    94	                            $payload['item_name'] = $payload['item_name'] ?: 'Product variant';
    95	                        } else {
    96	                            $payload['print_job_id'] = $jobs[array_rand($jobs)];
    97	                            $payload['item_name'] = $payload['item_name'] ?: 'Print job';
    98	                        }
    99	                    } elseif ($canUseVariant) {
   100	                        $payload['product_variant_id'] = $variants[array_rand($variants)];
   101	                        $payload['item_name'] = $payload['item_name'] ?: 'Product variant';
   102	                    } elseif ($canUseJob) {
   103	                        $payload['print_job_id'] = $jobs[array_rand($jobs)];
   104	                        $payload['item_name'] = $payload['item_name'] ?: 'Print job';
   105	                    } else {
   106	                        // No hay variantes ni jobs: evitamos crear items inválidos
   107	                        $this->command?->warn('No hay product_variants ni print_jobs. Se omiten items.');
   108	                        break;
   109	                    }
   110	
   111	                    OrderItem::create($payload);
   112	                }
   113	
   114	                //Recalcular totales snapshot desde items reales
   115	                $subtotal = OrderItem::query()
   116	                    ->where('order_id', $order->id)
   117	                    ->sum('subtotal');
   118	
   119	                $tax = 0;
   120	                $shipping = 0;
   121	                $total = $subtotal + $tax + $shipping;
   122	
   123	                $order->update([
   124	                    'subtotal' => $subtotal,
   125	                    'tax' => $tax,
   126	                    'shipping_cost' => $shipping,
   127	                    'total' => $total,
   128	                ]);
   129	            }
   130	        }
   131	    }
   132	}

============================================================
END FILE: database/seeders/DevOrdersSeeder.php
============================================================

BEGIN FILE: database/seeders/DevStreetsSeeder.php
============================================================
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\City;
     6	use App\Models\Street;
     7	use Illuminate\Database\Console\Seeds\WithoutModelEvents;
     8	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     9	
    10	class DevStreetsSeeder extends Seeder
    11	{
    12	    /**
    13	     * Run the database seeds.
    14	     */
    15	    public function run(): void
    16	    {
    17	        /**
    18	         * Calles mínimas para desarrollo.
    19	         * No pretende ser un dataset real.
    20	         * Objetivo: permitir creación de direcciones y pedidos.
    21	         */
    22	
    23	// Municipios reales existentes en la BD
    24	        $targetCities = [
    25	            'Madrid',
    26	            'Barcelona',
    27	            'Valencia',
    28	            'Sevilla',
    29	            'Alicante/Alacant',
    30	        ];
    31	
    32	        $cities = City::whereIn('name', $targetCities)
    33	            ->get()
    34	            ->keyBy('name');
    35	
    36	        // Calles mínimas por ciudad (DEV)
    37	        $streetsByCity = [
    38	
    39	            'Madrid' => [
    40	                ['name' => 'Gran Vía', 'street_type' => 'avenue'],
    41	            ],
    42	
    43	            'Barcelona' => [
    44	                ['name' => 'Avinguda Diagonal', 'street_type' => 'avenue'],
    45	            ],
    46	
    47	            'Valencia' => [
    48	                ['name' => 'Carrer de Colón', 'street_type' => 'street'],
    49	            ],
    50	
    51	            'Sevilla' => [
    52	                ['name' => 'Avenida de la Constitución', 'street_type' => 'avenue'],
    53	            ],
    54	
    55	            'Alicante/Alacant' => [
    56	                ['name' => 'Avenida Alfonso X el Sabio', 'street_type' => 'avenue'],
    57	            ],
    58	
    59	            // Aquí podrás añadir más ciudades/calles si lo necesitas
    60	        ];
    61	
    62	        foreach ($streetsByCity as $cityName => $streets) {
    63	
    64	            if (! $cities->has($cityName)) {
    65	                $this->command?->warn("Ciudad no encontrada: {$cityName}");
    66	                continue;
    67	            }
    68	
    69	            $city = $cities->get($cityName);
    70	
    71	            foreach ($streets as $streetData) {
    72	                Street::updateOrCreate(
    73	                    [
    74	                        'name' => $streetData['name'],
    75	                        'city_id' => $city->id,
    76	                    ],
    77	                    [
    78	                        'street_type' => $streetData['street_type'] ?? null,
    79	                    ]
    80	                );
    81	            }
    82	        }
    83	    }
    84	}

  1251	
  1252	            // Seguridad: comprobar que la provincia existe
  1253	            if (! $provinces->has($normalizedProvinceName)) {
  1254	                $this->command?->warn("Provincia no encontrada: {$provinceName}");

  1256	            }
  1257	
  1258	            $province = $provinces->get($normalizedProvinceName);
  1259	
  1260	
  1261	            foreach ($cities as $cityData) {
  1262	                City::updateOrCreate(
  1263	                    [
  1264	                        'province_id' => $province->id,
  1265	                        'name'        => $cityData['name'],
  1266	                    ],
  1267	                    [
  1268	                        'postal_code' => $cityData['postal_code'] ?? null,
  1269	                    ]

   115	                </dd>
   116	            </div>
   117	
   118	            <div>
   119	                <dt class="text-gray-900">Creado</dt>
   120	                <dd class="mt-1 text-gray-500">{{ $printJob->created_at->format('d/m/Y H:i') }}</dd>
   121	            </div>
   122	
   123	            <div>
   124	                <dt class="text-gray-900">Actualizado</dt>
   125	                <dd class="mt-1 text-gray-500">{{ $printJob->updated_at->format('d/m/Y H:i') }}</dd>
   126	            </div>
   127	        </dl>
   128	
   129	        {{-- Este botón (solo-Admin) simula disparar el pricing real del printJob, lo añade al carrito del
   130	         propietario y permite validar el flujo end-to-end sin frontend--}}
   131	
   132	        @if(
   133	    in_array($printJob->status->value, ['draft', 'priced'], true)
   134	    )
   135	            <div class="px-6 py-5 border-t bg-gray-50">
   136	                <form method="POST"
   137	                      action="{{ route('admin.users.cart.items.print-jobs.store', [$printJob->user_id, $printJob]) }}">
   138	                    @csrf
   139	
   140	                    <button type="submit"
   141	                            class="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm rounded hover:bg-red-500">
   142	                        Añadir al carrito (calcular precio)
   143	                    </button>
   144	
   145	                    <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   146	                        El precio se calculará automáticamente antes de añadir el trabajo al carrito.
   147	                    </p>
   148	                </form>
   149	            </div>
   150	        @endif
   151	
   152	
   153	        <div class="px-6 py-5 border-t">
   154	            <h3 class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">Pricing breakdown</h3>
   155	            <div class="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-700">
   156	                @if(!empty($printJob->pricing_breakdown))
   157	                    <pre class="bg-gray-50 border rounded p-3 overflow-auto">{{ json_encode($printJob->pricing_breakdown, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) }}</pre>

    57	                            @if($item->product_variant_id && !$item->print_job_id)
    58	                                Producto
    59	                            @elseif(!$item->product_variant_id && $item->print_job_id)
    60	                                Servicio impresión
    61	                            @else
    62	                                Inválido (XOR)
    63	                            @endif
    64	                        </td>
    65	
    66	                        <td class="px-3 py-2">
    67	                            @if($item->product_variant_id && !$item->print_job_id)
    68	                                <a class="underline text-gray-700 hover:text-gray-900"
    69	                                   href="{{ route('admin.products.variants.show', [optional($item->productVariant)->product_id, $item->productVariant]) }}">
    70	                                    Variant #{{ $item->product_variant_id }}
    71	                                </a>
    72	                            @elseif(!$item->product_variant_id && $item->print_job_id)
    73	                                <a class="underline text-gray-700 hover:text-gray-900"
    74	                                   href="{{ route('admin.print-files.jobs.show', [optional($item->printJob)->print_file_id, $item->printJob]) }}">
    75	                                    PrintJob #{{ $item->print_job_id }}
    76	                                </a>
    77	                            @else
    78	                                —
    79	                            @endif
    80	                        </td>
    81	
    82	                        <td class="px-3 py-2 text-right">{{ $item->quantity }}</td>
    83	                        <td class="px-3 py-2 text-right">{{ number_format((float) $item->unit_price, 2) }} €</td>
    84	                        <td class="px-3 py-2 text-right">{{ number_format((float) $item->subtotal, 2) }} €</td>
    85	                    </tr>
    86	                @empty
    87	                    <tr>

BEGIN FILE: resources/views/components/modal.blade.php
============================================================
     1	@props([
     2	    'name',
     3	    'show' => false,
     4	    'maxWidth' => '2xl'
     5	])
     6	
     7	@php
     8	$maxWidth = [
     9	    'sm' => 'sm:max-w-sm',
    10	    'md' => 'sm:max-w-md',
    11	    'lg' => 'sm:max-w-lg',
    12	    'xl' => 'sm:max-w-xl',
    13	    '2xl' => 'sm:max-w-2xl',
    14	][$maxWidth];
    15	@endphp
    16	
    17	<div
    18	    x-data="{
    19	        show: @js($show),
    20	        focusables() {
    21	            // All focusable element types...
    22	            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'
    23	            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]
    24	                // All non-disabled elements...
    25	                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))
    26	        },
    27	        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },
    28	        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },
    29	        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },
    30	        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },
    31	        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },
    32	        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },
    33	    }"
    34	    x-init="$watch('show', value => {
    35	        if (value) {
    36	            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');
    37	            {{ $attributes->has('focusable') ? 'setTimeout(() => firstFocusable().focus(), 100)' : '' }}
    38	        } else {
    39	            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');
    40	        }
    41	    })"
    42	    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = true : null"
    43	    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = false : null"
    44	    x-on:close.stop="show = false"
    45	    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"
    46	    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"
    47	    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"
    48	    x-show="show"
    49	    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"
    50	    style="display: {{ $show ? 'block' : 'none' }};"
    51	>
    52	    <div
    53	        x-show="show"
    54	        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"
    55	        x-on:click="show = false"
    56	        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
    57	        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"
    58	        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"
    59	        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
    60	        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"
    61	        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"
    62	    >
    63	        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500 opacity-75"></div>
    64	    </div>
    65	
    66	    <div
    67	        x-show="show"
    68	        class="mb-6 bg-white rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-xl transform transition-all sm:w-full {{ $maxWidth }} sm:mx-auto"
    69	        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
    70	        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
    71	        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
    72	        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
    73	        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
    74	        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
    75	    >
    76	        {{ $slot }}
    77	    </div>
    78	</div>

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     4	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
     5	
     6	use App\Http\Controllers\Api\AuthController;
     7	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\RegisteredUserController;
     8	
     9	use App\Http\Controllers\Api\Catalog\ProductController as CatalogProductController;
    10	use App\Http\Controllers\Api\Catalog\ProductVariantController as CatalogProductVariantController;

    12	use App\Http\Controllers\Api\PrintFileController as UserPrintFileController;
    13	
    14	// Auth (API / SPA)
    15	Route::middleware('guest')->group(function () {
    16	    Route::post('/register', [RegisteredUserController::class, 'store']);
    17	    Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login']);
    18	    Route::post('/token-login', [AuthController::class, 'tokenLogin']);
    19	
    20	});
    21	
    22	Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
    23	    Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);
    24	
    25	    Route::get('/me', function (Request $request) {
    26	        return $request->user();
    27	    });
    28	});
    29	
    30	// Catálogo público (sin login)
    31	Route::prefix('catalog')->group(function () {

    34

    36	    Route::get('/products/{product}/variants/{variant}', [CatalogProductVariantController::class, 'show']);
    37	});
    38	
    39	// PrintFiles del usuario (con login)

    42	    Route::post('/print-files', [UserPrintFileController::class, 'store']);

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\AuthenticatedSessionController;
     4	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\ConfirmablePasswordController;
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationNotificationController;
     6	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationPromptController;
     7	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\NewPasswordController;
     8	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordController;
     9	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordResetLinkController;
    10	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\RegisteredUserController;
    11	use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\VerifyEmailController;
    12	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
    13

    15	    Route::get('register', [RegisteredUserController::class, 'create'])
    16	        ->name('register');
    17

    19	
    20	    Route::get('login', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'create'])
    21	        ->name('login');
    22	
    23	    Route::post('login', [AuthenticatedSessionController::class, 'store']);
    24	
    25	    Route::get('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'create'])
    26	        ->name('password.request');
    27	
    28	    Route::post('forgot-password', [PasswordResetLinkController::class, 'store'])
    29	        ->name('password.email');
    30	
    31	    Route::get('reset-password/{token}', [NewPasswordController::class, 'create'])
    32	        ->name('password.reset');
    33	
    34	    Route::post('reset-password', [NewPasswordController::class, 'store'])
    35	        ->name('password.store');
    36	});
    37

    44	        ->name('verification.verify');

    47	        ->middleware('throttle:6,1')
    48	        ->name('verification.send');
    49

    51	        ->name('password.confirm');
    52	
    53	    Route::post('confirm-password', [ConfirmablePasswordController::class, 'store']);
    54	
    55	    Route::put('password', [PasswordController::class, 'update'])->name('password.update');

    86	        Route::get('/print-files', [PrintFileController::class, 'index'])
    87	            ->name('print-files.index');
    88	        Route::get('/print-files/create', [PrintFileController::class, 'create'])
    89	            ->name('print-files.create');
    90	        Route::post('/print-files', [PrintFileController::class, 'store'])
    91	            ->name('print-files.store');
    92	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/download', [PrintFileController::class, 'download'])
    93	            ->name('print-files.download');
    94	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}', [PrintFileController::class, 'show'])
    95	            ->name('print-files.show');
    96	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/edit', [PrintFileController::class, 'edit'])
    97	            ->name('print-files.edit');
    98	        Route::put('/print-files/{printFile}', [PrintFileController::class, 'update'])
    99	            ->name('print-files.update');
   100	        Route::delete('/print-files/{printFile}', [PrintFileController::class, 'destroy'])
   101	            ->name('print-files.destroy');
   102	
   103	        // CRUD de PrintJobs (anidado bajo PrintFiles)
   104	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs', [PrintJobController::class, 'index'])
   105	            ->name('print-files.jobs.index');
   106	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/create', [PrintJobController::class, 'create'])
   107	            ->name('print-files.jobs.create');
   108	        Route::post('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs', [PrintJobController::class, 'store'])
   109	            ->name('print-files.jobs.store');
   110	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}', [PrintJobController::class, 'show'])
   111	            ->name('print-files.jobs.show');
   112	        Route::get('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}/edit', [PrintJobController::class, 'edit'])
   113	            ->name('print-files.jobs.edit');
   114	        Route::put('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}', [PrintJobController::class, 'update'])
   115	            ->name('print-files.jobs.update');
   116	        Route::delete('/print-files/{printFile}/jobs/{printJob}', [PrintJobController::class, 'destroy'])
   117	            ->name('print-files.jobs.destroy');
   118	
   119	        //CRUD de User
   120	        Route::resource('users', UserController::class)
   121	            ->only(['index', 'show', 'edit', 'update']);
   122	
   123	        // CRUD de Addresses (anidado bajo Users)
   124	        Route::get('/users/{user}/addresses', [AddressController::class, 'index'])
   125	            ->name('users.addresses.index');
   126	        Route::get('/users/{user}/addresses/{address}', [AddressController::class, 'show'])
   127	            ->name('users.addresses.show');
   128	        Route::get('/users/{user}/addresses/{address}/edit', [AddressController::class, 'edit'])
   129	            ->name('users.addresses.edit');
   130	        Route::put('/users/{user}/addresses/{address}', [AddressController::class, 'update'])
   131	            ->name('users.addresses.update');
   132	        Route::delete('/users/{user}/addresses/{address}', [AddressController::class, 'destroy'])
   133	            ->name('users.addresses.destroy');
   134	
   135	        //CRUD de Cart
   136	        Route::get('/users/{user}/cart', [UserCartController::class, 'show'])
   137	            ->name('users.cart.show');
   138	        Route::post('/users/{user}/cart/items/product-variants/{variant}', [UserCartController::class, 'addProductVariant'])
   139	            ->name('users.cart.items.product-variants.store');
   140	        Route::post('/users/{user}/cart/items/print-jobs/{printJob}', [UserCartController::class, 'addPrintJob'])
   141	            ->name('users.cart.items.print-jobs.store');
   142	        Route::post('/users/{user}/cart/checkout', [UserCartController::class, 'checkout'])
   143	            ->name('users.cart.checkout');
   144	
   145	        //CRUD de Order
   146	            //Solo lectura para el acceso desde el panel
   147	        Route::get('/orders', [OrderController::class, 'globalIndex'])
   148	            ->name('orders.index');
   149	        Route::get('/orders/{order}', [OrderController::class, 'globalShow'])
   150	            ->name('orders.show');
   151	        Route::get('/orders/{order}/edit', [OrderController::class, 'edit'])
   152	            ->name('orders.edit');
   153	        Route::put('/orders/{order}', [OrderController::class, 'update'])
   154	            ->name('orders.update');
   155	
   156	            //Resto de rutas anidadas a User
   157	        Route::get('/users/{user}/orders', [OrderController::class, 'userIndex'])
   158	            ->name('users.orders.index');
   159	        Route::get('/users/{user}/orders/{order}', [OrderController::class, 'userShow'])
   160	            ->name('users.orders.show');
   161	
   162	
   163	
   164	
   165	    });
   166	
   167	
   168	require __DIR__.'/auth.php';

END FILE: routes/web.php
